## Blind spots

- LEXICAL: a table named through a constant (`.from(TABLE)`), an RPC or a trigger is not seen as a write; a writer reached only through a generic helper is attributed to the helper. A guard that only READS source files (a census) names no runtime survivor and is `guard-only` by design.
- A trigger is the nearest entry point over static AND dynamic imports (≤ 8 hops) — it proves a door can reach the survivor, not that every run does; the named proof carries the behaviour.
- `partial` / `writes-unread` / `untriggered` / `registry-only` are FINDINGS to triage, not verdicts of brokenness: a pull-based reader, a reader in SQL / a view, or an external consumer is invisible here.
